===== scripts/analysis/cop_entry_profile.py =====
   1: """
   2: H-ENTRY-01 (D1.1) â€” Perfil intradia DESCRIPTIVO de USD/COP (M5) por bloque de 30 min.
   3: =====================================================================================
   4: 
   5: DESCRIPTIVO, no comparativo: este script NO evalua ninguna regla de entrada.
   6: Solo caracteriza el costo/friccion intradia de la sesion COP (8:00-12:55 COT)
   7: para fundamentar el prior economico del pre-registro H-ENTRY-01.
   8: 
   9: LINEA ROJA (quant-constitution Â§1): corre SOLO sobre anos de DISENO 2020-2024.
  10: Un assert duro rechaza cualquier intento de tocar 2025+ (el OOS/forward queda
  11: virgen para la comparacion pre-registrada, que corre el operador tras sellar
  12: el pre-registro).
  13: 
  14: Datos: usdcop_m5_ohlcv (symbol='USD/COP'), TIMESTAMPTZ en UTC -> convertido a
  15: America/Bogota (data-governance golden rule). Sesion 8:00-12:55 COT, Lun-Vie.
  16: 
  17: ADVERTENCIA DE CALIDAD DE DATOS (verificada 2026-07-21 contra la DB):
  18:   2020-2022: el 100% de las barras M5 son "flat" (high=low=open=close) â€” son
  19:   snapshots de precio, no barras OHLC reales. 2023: ~90% flat; 2024: ~84% flat.
  20:   => (high-low)/close por barra es DEGENERADO como proxy de spread pre-2023.
  21:   Por eso el proxy primario de costo aqui es el RANGO DE CLOSES INTRA-BLOQUE
  22:   (max(close)-min(close))/close_final por (dia, bloque), que si captura el
  23:   movimiento realizado dentro de la media hora, y la volatilidad de retornos
  24:   5m close-to-close. El (high-low)/close por barra se reporta igualmente,
  25:   con su flat_share al lado, para que nadie lo lea sin el caveat.
  26: 
  27: Metricas por bloque de 30 min (8:00, 8:30, ..., 12:30; el ultimo cubre
  28: 12:30-12:55), para (a) todos los dias y (b) solo lunes (dia de entrada H5):
  29:   - bar_range_bp_mean : mean( 1e4*(high-low)/close ) por barra  [con flat_share]
  30:   - flat_share        : fraccion de barras high==low (caveat del anterior)
  31:   - close_range_bp_*  : 1e4*(max(c)-min(c))/c_last por (dia,bloque) -> mean/median
  32:   - ret5m_vol_bp      : std de log-returns 5m close-to-close (intra-dia) * 1e4
  33:   - abs_ret5m_bp_mean : mean(|log-return 5m|) * 1e4
  34:   - n_bars, n_days
  35: 
  36: Salida (JSON sin Infinity/NaN via sanitize + tabla legible):
  37:   .claude/evidence/cop_entry_profile/2026-07-22/
  38:     â”œâ”€â”€ cop_entry_profile.json
  39:     â”œâ”€â”€ cop_entry_profile.txt
  40:     â””â”€â”€ generator_script.py   (copia de este script)
  41: 
  42: Uso:
  43:   set -a; . ./.env; set +a
  44:   python scripts/analysis/cop_entry_profile.py
  45: 
  46: @contract H-ENTRY-01 / TAREA D1.1
  47: @date 2026-07-21
  48: """
  49: 
  50: import json
  51: import math
  52: import os
  53: import shutil
  54: import sys
  55: from pathlib import Path
  56: 
  57: import numpy as np
  58: import pandas as pd
  59: 
  60: PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
  61: sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
  62: 
  63: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  64: # Design-years hard line (quant-constitution Â§1)
  65: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  66: DESIGN_START = "2020-01-01"
  67: DESIGN_END_EXCL = "2025-01-01"   # exclusive â€” 2025+ is OOS/forward, NEVER touched here
  68: 
  69: EVIDENCE_DIR = PROJECT_ROOT / ".claude" / "evidence" / "cop_entry_profile" / "2026-07-22"
  70: 
  71: SESSION_START = "08:00"
  72: SESSION_END = "12:55"            # inclusive last bar
  73: BLOCKS = ["08:00", "08:30", "09:00", "09:30", "10:00",
  74:           "10:30", "11:00", "11:30", "12:00", "12:30"]
  75: 
  76: # Ventanas de referencia del pre-registro (solo etiquetas descriptivas aqui)
  77: FIRST_BLOCK = "08:00"                    # primera media hora
  78: TWAP_BLOCKS = ["09:30", "10:00"]         # ventana TWAP candidata 9:30-10:30
  79: 
  80: 
  81: def _sanitize(obj):
  82:     """JSON safety (strategy-contract): Infinity/NaN -> None, numpy -> python."""
  83:     if isinstance(obj, dict):
  84:         return {k: _sanitize(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
  85:     if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
  86:         return [_sanitize(v) for v in obj]
  87:     if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):
  88:         return int(obj)
  89:     if isinstance(obj, (np.floating, float)):
  90:         f = float(obj)
  91:         return None if (math.isnan(f) or math.isinf(f)) else round(f, 6)
  92:     if isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):
  93:         return bool(obj)
  94:     return obj
  95: 
  96: 
  97: def load_m5_design_years() -> pd.DataFrame:
  98:     import psycopg2
  99:     conn = psycopg2.connect(
 100:         host=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost"),
 101:         port=int(os.environ.get("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432")),
 102:         dbname=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_DB", "usdcop_trading"),
 103:         user=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_USER", "admin"),
 104:         password=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", ""),
 105:     )
 106:     # Bounds anchored in COT instants; conversion to America/Bogota in-query.
 107:     q = f"""
 108:         SELECT (time AT TIME ZONE 'America/Bogota') AS ts_cot,
 109:                open::float8, high::float8, low::float8, close::float8
 110:         FROM usdcop_m5_ohlcv
 111:         WHERE symbol = 'USD/COP'
 112:           AND time >= '{DESIGN_START} 00:00:00-05'
 113:           AND time <  '{DESIGN_END_EXCL} 00:00:00-05'
 114:         ORDER BY time
 115:     """
 116:     df = pd.read_sql(q, conn)
 117:     conn.close()
 118: 
 119:     df["ts_cot"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts_cot"])
 120:     df["date"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.date
 121:     df["year"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.year
 122:     df["dow"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.dayofweek
 123:     df["hhmm"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.strftime("%H:%M")
 124: 
 125:     # Session filter 8:00-12:55 COT (bars outside session are a data bug upstream)
 126:     df = df[(df["hhmm"] >= SESSION_START) & (df["hhmm"] <= SESSION_END)].copy()
 127: 
 128:     # HARD LINE: no bar beyond design years may survive into the profile.
 129:     assert df["year"].max() <= 2024, (
 130:         f"RED LINE VIOLATION: found year {df['year'].max()} > 2024 â€” "
 131:         "this profile is design-years (2020-2024) ONLY."
 132:     )
 133:     assert df["year"].min() >= 2020, f"unexpected year {df['year'].min()} < 2020"
 134: 
 135:     # 30-min block label (floor)
 136:     minute_block = (df["ts_cot"].dt.minute // 30) * 30
 137:     df["block"] = (df["ts_cot"].dt.hour.astype(str).str.zfill(2) + ":" +
 138:                    minute_block.astype(str).str.zfill(2))
 139: 
 140:     # Intra-day 5m log-returns (close-to-close), first bar of each day = NaN
 141:     df = df.sort_values("ts_cot").reset_index(drop=True)
 142:     df["log_close"] = np.log(df["close"])
 143:     df["ret5m"] = df.groupby("date")["log_close"].diff()
 144:     return df
 145: 
 146: 
 147: def profile_blocks(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:
 148:     """Per-30min-block descriptive metrics for the given subset of days."""
 149:     out = []
 150:     for block in BLOCKS:
 151:         b = df[df["block"] == block]
 152:         if b.empty:
 153:             out.append({"block": block, "n_bars": 0})
 154:             continue
 155:         # per-bar range proxy (degenerate pre-2023 â€” see flat_share)
 156:         bar_range_bp = 1e4 * (b["high"] - b["low"]) / b["close"]
 157:         flat_share = float((b["high"] == b["low"]).mean())
 158:         # per-(day,block) close-range proxy
 159:         g = b.groupby("date")["close"]
 160:         close_range_bp = 1e4 * (g.max() - g.min()) / g.last()
 161:         # 5m return stats (first bar of day excluded by construction)
 162:         rets = b["ret5m"].dropna()
 163:         out.append({
 164:             "block": block,
 165:             "n_bars": int(len(b)),
 166:             "n_days": int(b["date"].nunique()),
 167:             "bar_range_bp_mean": float(bar_range_bp.mean()),
 168:             "flat_share": flat_share,
 169:             "close_range_bp_mean": float(close_range_bp.mean()),
 170:             "close_range_bp_median": float(close_range_bp.median()),
 171:             "ret5m_vol_bp": float(rets.std(ddof=1) * 1e4) if len(rets) > 2 else None,
 172:             "abs_ret5m_bp_mean": float(rets.abs().mean() * 1e4) if len(rets) else None,
 173:         })
 174:     return out
 175: 
 176: 
 177: def first_vs_twap(df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
 178:     """Headline ratio: first half-hour (8:00-8:30) vs TWAP window (9:30-10:30).
 179: 
 180:     Equal-granularity comparison: the close-range for the TWAP window is the
 181:     average of its per-30min-block means (NOT the pooled 60-min range, which
 182:     would mechanically inflate the window and understate the ratio).
 183:     """
 184:     def _stats(sub, blocks):
 185:         # per-(day, 30min-block) close range, then mean â€” same horizon everywhere
 186:         cr = (sub.groupby(["date", "block"])["close"]
 187:                  .agg(["max", "min", "last"]))
 188:         cr_bp = 1e4 * (cr["max"] - cr["min"]) / cr["last"]
 189:         rets = sub["ret5m"].dropna()
 190:         return {
 191:             "close_range_bp_mean_per_30min": float(cr_bp.mean()) if len(cr_bp) else None,
 192:             "ret5m_vol_bp": float(rets.std(ddof=1) * 1e4) if len(rets) > 2 else None,
 193:             "n_days": int(sub["date"].nunique()),
 194:             "blocks": blocks,
 195:         }
 196: 
 197:     first = _stats(df[df["block"] == FIRST_BLOCK], [FIRST_BLOCK])
 198:     twap = _stats(df[df["block"].isin(TWAP_BLOCKS)], TWAP_BLOCKS)
 199:     ratios = {}
 200:     for k in ("close_range_bp_mean_per_30min", "ret5m_vol_bp"):
 201:         if first.get(k) and twap.get(k):
 202:             ratios[f"{k}_ratio_first_over_twap"] = first[k] / twap[k]
 203:     return {"first_0800_0830": first, "twap_0930_1030": twap, "ratios": ratios}
 204: 
 205: 
 206: def fmt_table(rows: list, title: str) -> str:
 207:     hdr = (f"{'block':>6} | {'n_bars':>6} | {'n_days':>6} | {'barRng_bp':>9} | "
 208:            f"{'flat%':>6} | {'closeRng_bp':>11} | {'medRng_bp':>9} | "
 209:            f"{'vol5m_bp':>8} | {'|r5m|_bp':>8}")
 210:     lines = [title, "=" * len(hdr), hdr, "-" * len(hdr)]
 211:     for r in rows:
 212:         if r.get("n_bars", 0) == 0:
 213:             lines.append(f"{r['block']:>6} | {'0':>6} | (no data)")
 214:             continue
 215:         def f(v, nd=2):
 216:             return "n/a" if v is None else f"{v:.{nd}f}"
 217:         lines.append(
 218:             f"{r['block']:>6} | {r['n_bars']:>6} | {r['n_days']:>6} | "
 219:             f"{f(r['bar_range_bp_mean']):>9} | {100*r['flat_share']:>5.1f}% | "
 220:             f"{f(r['close_range_bp_mean']):>11} | {f(r['close_range_bp_median']):>9} | "
 221:             f"{f(r['ret5m_vol_bp']):>8} | {f(r['abs_ret5m_bp_mean']):>8}"
 222:         )
 223:     return "\n".join(lines)
 224:

 232: 
 233:     mondays = df[df["dow"] == 0]
 234: 
 235:     result = {
 236:         "hypothesis": "H-ENTRY-01",
 237:         "task": "D1.1 descriptive intraday entry-cost profile (DESIGN YEARS ONLY)",
 238:         "generated_at": pd.Timestamp.now(tz="America/Bogota").isoformat(),
 239:         "design_window": {"start": DESIGN_START, "end_exclusive": DESIGN_END_EXCL},
 240:         "session_cot": f"{SESSION_START}-{SESSION_END}",
 241:         "source": "usdcop_m5_ohlcv (symbol='USD/COP'), TIMESTAMPTZ->America/Bogota",
 242:         "data_quality_warning": (
 243:             "2020-2022 M5 bars are 100% flat (high=low=open=close, price snapshots); "
 244:             "2023 ~90% flat, 2024 ~84% flat. Per-bar (high-low)/close is degenerate as "
 245:             "a spread proxy pre-2023 â€” primary cost proxies here are the intra-block "
 246:             "close-range and 5m close-to-close return volatility."
 247:         ),
 248:         "totals": {
 249:             "n_bars": int(len(df)),
 250:             "n_days": int(df["date"].nunique()),
 251:             "n_mondays": int(mondays["date"].nunique()),
 252:             "flat_bar_share": float(n_flat / len(df)),
 253:         },
 254:         "profile_all_days": profile_blocks(df),
 255:         "profile_mondays": profile_blocks(mondays),
 256:         "first_vs_twap": {
 257:             "all_days": first_vs_twap(df),

 271: 
 272:     tables = [

 274:         f"Generado: {result['generated_at']}   Sesion: 8:00-12:55 COT",
 275:         "",

 284:         json.dumps(_sanitize(result["first_vs_twap"]), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),

===== scripts/analysis/cop_entry_compare.py =====
   1: """
   2: H-ENTRY-01 (D1.2) â€” Comparacion PRE-REGISTRADA de regla de entrada del lunes (USD/COP H5).
   3: ==========================================================================================
   4: 
   5: *** ESTE SCRIPT NO SE EJECUTA HASTA SELLAR EL PRE-REGISTRO H-ENTRY-01. ***
   6: *** CADA EJECUCION REAL = +1 TRIAL en HYPOTHESIS-REGISTRY (quant-constitution Â§2). ***
   7: La corrida real exige el flag explicito --confirm-trial; sin el, el script se niega.
   8: --dry-run solo valida sintaxis/imports/conexion (LIMIT 10) y NO computa ninguna metrica.
   9: 
  10: REGLA DE ENTRADA AS-IS DEL MOTOR (documentada leyendo
  11: scripts/pipeline/train_and_export_smart_simple.py, 2026-07-21):
  12: 
  13:   - `_run_weekly_loop` (lineas ~377-386) y el loop de produccion (~625-634):
  14:         # Entry = Monday close
  15:         monday_row = df[df["date"] == monday_ts]
  16:         if monday_row.empty:            # lunes festivo/sin dato ->
  17:             monday_row = df[df["date"] >= monday_ts].iloc[:1]   # primer dia habil sig.
  18:         entry = float(monday_row["close"].iloc[0])
  19:     => La entrada es el CLOSE DIARIO del lunes (barra diaria = sesion 8:00-12:55 COT,
  20:        o sea el cierre ~12:55 COT), tomado del dataset diario del engine
  21:        (ForecastingDatasetLoader: DB usdcop_daily_ohlcv-first, seed parquet fallback).
  22:   - Si el lunes no existe (festivo), entra al close del primer dia habil siguiente.
  23:   - Costos modelados en `compute_pnl`: maker_fee (0 bps MEXC) + slippage (1 bps por
  24:     lado) sobre esa entrada; NO se modela ningun spread intradia adicional.
  25:   - Inconsistencia cosmetica documentada: el trade exportado lleva
  26:     `timestamp = <lunes> 09:00 COT` (linea ~433) aunque el PRECIO usado es el close
  27:     diario (~12:55 COT). El precio manda; el timestamp es solo presentacion.
  28:   - En vivo, la senal H5-L5 sale Lun 08:15 COT y el executor corre */30 8:00-12:55;
  29:     este script compara la regla del BACKTEST/export (la que produce los numeros de
  30:     aprobacion), no el timing del executor vivo.
  31: 
  32: REGLA CANDIDATA: TWAP 9:30-10:30 COT = promedio simple de los closes M5 con
  33: timestamp COT en [09:30, 10:25] (12 barras que cubren 9:30-10:30), del mismo lunes.
  34: 
  35: METRICA (pre-registrada):
  36:   improvement_bp = direction * (entry_engine - entry_twap) / entry_engine * 1e4
  37:   (>0 = la TWAP consigue mejor precio EN LA DIRECCION del trade; direction: LONG=+1,
  38:   SHORT=-1, reproducida con la MISMA cadena del motor: regime gate Hurst +
  39:   compute_momentum_signal sobre datos previos al lunes â€” Layers 1-2 as-is).
  40:   Limitacion declarada: el circuit breaker (path-dependent en PnL) NO se reproduce
  41:   para seleccionar semanas; se comparan todas las semanas que pasan gate+signal.
  42:   Tambien se reporta la version incondicional (todos los lunes, ambas direcciones
  43:   hipoteticas y |diff| bruto) como diagnostico secundario.
  44: 
  45:   Por aÃ±o y pooled: media, IC95 bootstrap con remuestreo por BLOQUE SEMANAL
  46:   (la observacion = 1 semana; 10,000 remuestras, seed=42, percentiles 2.5/97.5).
  47: 
  48: LINEA ROJA (quant-constitution Â§1): SOLO aÃ±os de DISEÃ‘O 2020-2024. Asserts duros
  49: rechazan cualquier aÃ±o > 2024 tanto en el query como en los datos cargados.
  50: El juez de confirmacion es el forward (>= sello del pre-registro), NUNCA este OOS.
  51: 
  52: Salida (solo corrida real):
  53:   .claude/evidence/cop_entry_compare/<YYYY-MM-DD>/
  54:     â”œâ”€â”€ cop_entry_compare.json   (sin Infinity/NaN)
  55:     â”œâ”€â”€ cop_entry_compare.txt
  56:     â””â”€â”€ generator_script.py
  57: 
  58: Uso:
  59:   set -a; . ./.env; set +a
  60:   python scripts/analysis/cop_entry_compare.py --dry-run          # smoke SIN computo
  61:   python scripts/analysis/cop_entry_compare.py --confirm-trial    # corrida real (+1 trial)
  62: 
  63: @contract H-ENTRY-01 / TAREA D1.2
  64: @date 2026-07-21
  65: """
  66: 
  67: import argparse
  68: import json
  69: import math
  70: import os
  71: import shutil
  72: import sys
  73: from datetime import date
  74: from pathlib import Path
  75: 
  76: import numpy as np
  77: import pandas as pd
  78: 
  79: PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
  80: sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
  81: 
  82: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83: # Design-years hard line (quant-constitution Â§1)
  84: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  85: DESIGN_YEARS = [2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024]
  86: DESIGN_START = "2020-01-01"
  87: DESIGN_END_EXCL = "2025-01-01"   # exclusive â€” 2025+ is OOS/forward, NEVER touched here
  88: 
  89: TWAP_HHMM_START = "09:30"
  90: TWAP_HHMM_END = "10:25"          # inclusive bar stamps covering 9:30-10:30 COT
  91: TWAP_MIN_BARS = 6                # else the Monday is skipped (recorded)
  92: 
  93: BOOTSTRAP_N = 10_000
  94: BOOTSTRAP_SEED = 42
  95: 
  96: 
  97: def _assert_design_only(years) -> None:
  98:     years = [int(y) for y in years]
  99:     assert years and max(years) <= 2024 and min(years) >= 2020, (
 100:         f"RED LINE VIOLATION: years {sorted(set(years))} outside design window "
 101:         "2020-2024. This script REFUSES to touch 2025+ (OOS/forward). "
 102:         "Confirmation judge is the forward period per the H-ENTRY-01 pre-registration."
 103:     )
 104: 
 105: 
 106: def _sanitize(obj):
 107:     """JSON safety (strategy-contract): Infinity/NaN -> None, numpy -> python."""
 108:     if isinstance(obj, dict):
 109:         return {k: _sanitize(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
 110:     if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
 111:         return [_sanitize(v) for v in obj]
 112:     if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):
 113:         return int(obj)
 114:     if isinstance(obj, (np.floating, float)):
 115:         f = float(obj)
 116:         return None if (math.isnan(f) or math.isinf(f)) else round(f, 6)
 117:     if isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):
 118:         return bool(obj)
 119:     return obj
 120: 
 121: 
 122: def _db_conn():
 123:     import psycopg2
 124:     return psycopg2.connect(
 125:         host=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost"),
 126:         port=int(os.environ.get("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432")),
 127:         dbname=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_DB", "usdcop_trading"),
 128:         user=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_USER", "admin"),
 129:         password=os.environ.get("POSTGRES_PASSWORD", ""),
 130:     )
 131: 
 132: 
 133: def load_m5_design_years(limit: int | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
 134:     """M5 bars, design years only, converted to America/Bogota (data-governance)."""
 135:     conn = _db_conn()
 136:     lim = f"LIMIT {int(limit)}" if limit else ""
 137:     q = f"""
 138:         SELECT (time AT TIME ZONE 'America/Bogota') AS ts_cot, close::float8
 139:         FROM usdcop_m5_ohlcv
 140:         WHERE symbol = 'USD/COP'
 141:           AND time >= '{DESIGN_START} 00:00:00-05'
 142:           AND time <  '{DESIGN_END_EXCL} 00:00:00-05'
 143:         ORDER BY time
 144:         {lim}
 145:     """
 146:     df = pd.read_sql(q, conn)
 147:     conn.close()
 148:     df["ts_cot"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts_cot"])
 149:     df["date"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.normalize()
 150:     df["hhmm"] = df["ts_cot"].dt.strftime("%H:%M")
 151:     if not limit:  # dry-run LIMIT 10 already inside bounds; full load re-asserted
 152:         _assert_design_only(df["ts_cot"].dt.year.unique())
 153:     return df
 154: 
 155: 
 156: def reproduce_engine_weeks(year: int):
 157:     """
 158:     Reproduce, AS-IS, which Mondays the engine trades and in which direction
 159:     (Layers 1-2 of _run_weekly_loop: regime gate + momentum signal), using the
 160:     engine's own config loader, dataset loader and signal functions.
 161:     Circuit breaker (Layer 3, path-dependent) intentionally NOT reproduced â€”
 162:     declared limitation in the pre-registration.
 163:     Returns (list of {monday, direction, engine_entry, engine_entry_date}, df_daily).
 164:     """
 165:     from scripts.pipeline.train_and_export_smart_simple import load_config, load_data
 166:     from src.forecasting.regime_gate import classify_regime
 167:     from src.forecasting.momentum_signal import compute_momentum_signal, SignalConfidence
 168: 
 169:     cfg = load_config()
 170:     df, _feature_cols = load_data()
 171: 
 172:     _assert_design_only([year])
 173:     test = df[(df["date"] >= pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")) &
 174:               (df["date"] <= pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-12-31"))].copy()
 175:     test["dow"] = test["date"].dt.dayofweek
 176:     mondays = sorted(test[test["dow"] == 0]["date"].unique())
 177: 
 178:     weeks = []
 179:     for monday in mondays:
 180:         monday_ts = pd.Timestamp(monday)
 181:         prior = df[df["date"] < monday_ts]
 182:         daily_rets = prior["return_1d"].dropna().values
 183:         regime = classify_regime(daily_rets.tolist(), cfg["regime_gate"])
 184:         if regime.sizing_factor <= 0:
 185:             continue
 186:         closes = prior["close"].dropna().values
 187:         if len(closes) < 52:
 188:             continue
 189:         signal = compute_momentum_signal(closes, regime.state.value)
 190:         if signal.direction is None or signal.confidence == SignalConfidence.SKIP:
 191:             continue
 192: 
 193:         # Engine entry AS-IS: Monday DAILY close; holiday fallback = next bday close
 194:         row = df[df["date"] == monday_ts]
 195:         if row.empty:
 196:             m2 = df["date"] >= monday_ts
 197:             if not m2.any():
 198:                 continue
 199:             row = df[m2].iloc[:1]
 200:         weeks.append({
 201:             "monday": monday_ts,
 202:             "direction": int(signal.direction),
 203:             "engine_entry": float(row["close"].iloc[0]),
 204:             "engine_entry_date": pd.Timestamp(row["date"].iloc[0]),
 205:         })
 206:     return weeks
 207: 
 208: 
 209: def twap_entry(m5: pd.DataFrame, day: pd.Timestamp):
 210:     """TWAP 9:30-10:30 COT = simple mean of M5 closes stamped [09:30, 10:25]."""
 211:     bars = m5[(m5["date"] == day.normalize()) &
 212:               (m5["hhmm"] >= TWAP_HHMM_START) & (m5["hhmm"] <= TWAP_HHMM_END)]
 213:     if len(bars) < TWAP_MIN_BARS:
 214:         return None, int(len(bars))
 215:     return float(bars["close"].mean()), int(len(bars))
 216: 
 217: 
 218: def bootstrap_ci(values: np.ndarray, n_boot: int = BOOTSTRAP_N,
 219:                  seed: int = BOOTSTRAP_SEED):
 220:     """Weekly-block bootstrap: the observation unit IS the week (one entry per
 221:     Monday), so resampling weeks i.i.d. == block bootstrap with weekly blocks."""
 222:     if len(values) < 3:
 223:         return None, None
 224:     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
 225:     idx = rng.integers(0, len(values), size=(n_boot, len(values)))
 226:     means = values[idx].mean(axis=1)
 227:     return float(np.percentile(means, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(means, 97.5))
 228: 
 229: 
 230: def run_compare() -> dict:
 231:     _assert_design_only(DESIGN_YEARS)
 232:     m5 = load_m5_design_years()
 233: 
 234:     rows, skipped = [], []
 235:     for year in DESIGN_YEARS:
 236:         for w in reproduce_engine_weeks(year):
 237:             entry_day = w["engine_entry_date"]
 238:             twap, nbars = twap_entry(m5, entry_day)
 239:             if twap is None:
 240:                 skipped.append({"monday": str(w["monday"].date()),
 241:                                 "reason": f"twap_window_bars={nbars} < {TWAP_MIN_BARS}"})
 242:                 continue
 243:             eng = w["engine_entry"]
 244:             rows.append({
 245:                 "year": year,
 246:                 "monday": str(w["monday"].date()),
 247:                 "entry_date": str(entry_day.date()),
 248:                 "direction": w["direction"],
 249:                 "engine_entry": eng,
 250:                 "twap_entry": twap,
 251:                 "improvement_bp": w["direction"] * (eng - twap) / eng * 1e4,
 252:                 "abs_diff_bp": abs(eng - twap) / eng * 1e4,
 253:             })
 254: 
 255:     dfw = pd.DataFrame(rows)
 256: 
 257:     def _summary(sub: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
 258:         vals = sub["improvement_bp"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
 259:         lo, hi = bootstrap_ci(vals)
 260:         return {
 261:             "n_weeks": int(len(sub)),
 262:             "improvement_bp_mean": float(vals.mean()) if len(vals) else None,
 263:             "improvement_bp_ci95": [lo, hi],
 264:             "ci95_excludes_zero": (lo is not None and (lo > 0 or hi < 0)),
 265:             "abs_diff_bp_mean": float(sub["abs_diff_bp"].mean()) if len(sub) else None,
 266:             "share_twap_better": float((sub["improvement_bp"] > 0).mean()) if len(sub) else None,
 267:         }
 268: 
 269:     result = {
 270:         "hypothesis": "H-ENTRY-01",
 271:         "task": "D1.2 pre-registered entry-rule comparison (DESIGN YEARS ONLY)",
 272:         "generated_at": pd.Timestamp.now(tz="America/Bogota").isoformat(),
 273:         "design_window": {"start": DESIGN_START, "end_exclusive": DESIGN_END_EXCL},
 274:         "rule_engine_asis": "Monday DAILY close (~12:55 COT session close); holiday -> next bday close",
 275:         "rule_candidate": f"TWAP mean of M5 closes stamped [{TWAP_HHMM_START},{TWAP_HHMM_END}] COT",
 276:         "metric": "direction * (entry_engine - entry_twap) / entry_engine * 1e4  (>0 = TWAP better)",
 277:         "bootstrap": {"kind": "weekly-block (obs=week)", "n": BOOTSTRAP_N, "seed": BOOTSTRAP_SEED},
 278:         "limitations": [
 279:             "circuit breaker (path-dependent) not reproduced for week selection",
 280:             "M5 2020-2022 bars are price snapshots (flat OHLC); closes are valid, ranges are not",
 281:         ],
 282:         "by_year": {str(y): _summary(dfw[dfw["year"] == y]) for y in DESIGN_YEARS},
 283:         "pooled": _summary(dfw),
 284:         "skipped_weeks": skipped,
 285:         "weeks": rows,
 286:         "trial_accounting": "THIS RUN = +1 trial for H-ENTRY-01 in "
 287:                             ".claude/specs/assets/usdcop/HYPOTHESIS-REGISTRY.md â€” log it.",
 288:     }
 289:     return result
 290:

 306:           f"({df['ts_cot'].min()} .. {df['ts_cot'].max()} COT)")

 308:           "(and counts +1 trial in the H-ENTRY-01 registry).")

 344:                     if len(embi_ser) > 260 else np.nan)

 346:         res_ser = resint.loc[:asof - pd.Timedelta(days=45)].dropna()
 347:         res_z = (float((res_ser.iloc[-1] - res_ser.tail(12).mean())
 348:                        / (res_ser.tail(12).std() + 1e-9))
 349:                  if len(res_ser) > 12 else np.nan)
 350:         # f3 Monday gap-tail conditional prob given VIX tercile (identical to screen)
 351:         vix_t1 = float(vix.loc[:asof].iloc[-1]) if len(vix.loc[:asof]) else np.nan
 352:         prev_mon = prev[prev["dow"] == 0].tail(252)
 353:         if len(prev_mon) > 30 and not np.isnan(vix_t1):
 354:             vix_al = vix.reindex(prev_mon["date"], method="ffill")
 355:             ter = pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
 356:             my_ter = 1
 357:             if len(pd.unique(ter.dropna())) == 3:
 358:                 _cut = pd.cut([vix_t1], bins=pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, retbins=True,
 359:                               duplicates="drop")[1], labels=False, include_lowest=True)[0]
 360:                 if pd.isna(_cut):
 361:                     _cut = 0 if vix_t1 < float(vix_al.min()) else 2
 362:                 my_ter = int(_cut)
 363:             mask = (ter == my_ter).to_numpy()
 364:             thr = prev_mon["gap"].quantile(0.90)
 365:             f3 = float((prev_mon["gap"].to_numpy()[mask] > thr).mean()) if mask.sum() > 5 else np.nan
 366:         else:
 367:             f3 = np.nan
 368:         # persistence: q90 of the trailing 252 realized-5d-range values strictly < t
 369:         rng5_hist = prev["rng5"].dropna().tail(252)
 370:         pers = float(rng5_hist.quantile(0.90)) if len(rng5_hist) >= 252 else np.nan
 371:         rows.append({"monday": wk.start_time, "last_date": g["date"].iloc[-1],
 372:                      "range_pct": float(range_pct), "persistence": pers,
 373:                      "f2": float(vv) if np.isfinite(vv) else np.nan,
 374:                      "f3": f3, "f4": embi_acc, "f5": res_z})
 375:     W = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("monday").reset_index(drop=True)
 376: 
 377:     feat_cols = ["f2", "f3", "f4", "f5"]
 378:     design_start = pd.Timestamp("2020-01-01")
 379:     design_end = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
 380:     ceilings, q90_hist = {}, []          # q90_hist: (monday, q90_hat) for the causal median
 381:     n_qr_fallback = n_inactive = n_weeks = 0
 382:     for i in range(len(W)):
 383:         t = W["monday"].iloc[i]
 384:         if t < design_start:
 385:             continue
 386:         n_weeks += 1
 387:         row_feats = W[feat_cols].iloc[i].to_numpy(dtype=float)
 388:         train = W[(W["last_date"] < t) & W["range_pct"].notna()].dropna(subset=feat_cols)
 389:         qr_pred = np.nan
 390:         if np.isfinite(row_feats).all() and len(train) >= 52:
 391:             try:
 392:                 X = sm.add_constant(train[feat_cols].to_numpy(dtype=float))
 393:                 qr = sm.QuantReg(train["range_pct"].to_numpy(dtype=float), X).fit(q=0.90)
 394:                 qr_pred = float(np.asarray(qr.params) @ np.concatenate(([1.0], row_feats)))
 395:             except Exception:
 396:                 qr_pred = np.nan
 397:         pers = W["persistence"].iloc[i]
 398:         if np.isfinite(pers) and np.isfinite(qr_pred):
 399:             q90_hat = 0.5 * pers + 0.5 * qr_pred
 400:         elif np.isfinite(pers):
 401:             q90_hat = float(pers)
 402:             n_qr_fallback += 1
 403:         else:
 404:             q90_hat = np.nan
 405:         # causal design median: q90_hat values with design_start <= s < min(t, design_end)
 406:         med_vals = [v for (s, v) in q90_hist if s < min(t, design_end) and np.isfinite(v)]
 407:         if np.isfinite(q90_hat):
 408:             q90_hist.append((t, q90_hat))
 409:         if len(med_vals) >= 26 and np.isfinite(q90_hat) and q90_hat > 0:
 410:             med = float(np.median(med_vals))
 411:             ceilings[t] = 1.5 * float(np.clip(med / q90_hat, 0.5, 1.0))
 412:         else:
 413:             ceilings[t] = 1.5   # warm-up / fail-safe: inactive, never raises above 1.5
 414:             n_inactive += 1
 415:     meta = {
 416:         "n_weeks": n_weeks, "n_qr_fallback": n_qr_fallback, "n_ceiling_inactive": n_inactive,
 417:         "median_design_final": (float(np.median([v for (s, v) in q90_hist
 418:                                                  if s < design_end and np.isfinite(v)]))
 419:                                 if q90_hist else None),
 420:         "ceiling_mean": float(np.mean(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
 421:         "ceiling_min": float(np.min(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
 422:     }
 423:     return ceilings, meta

 430: def _run_weekly_loop(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=False):
 431:     """
 432:     Smart Simple v3.0 core loop â€” Momentum Adaptativo + RÃ©gimen Gate.
 433: 
 434:     NO ML model. Signal = multi-horizon momentum (ret_10d/20d/50d).
 435:     Architecture:
 436:       Layer 1: Regime gate (Hurst) â†’ decides WHETHER to trade
 437:       Layer 2: Momentum signal â†’ decides WHICH direction
 438:       Layer 3: Execution (Half-Kelly, ATR TP, Effective HS, CB, DL)
 439:     """

 444:     mondays = sorted(test_data[test_data["dow"] == 0]["date"].unique())
 445: 
 446:     if not len(mondays):
 447:         return {"trades": [], "equity": 10000.0, "metrics": {},
 448:                 "skipped_weeks": [], "week_data": []}
 449: 
 450:     equity = 10000.0

 590:         )

 631:             "regime": regime.state.value,

 633:         }
 634:         trades.append(trade_dict)
 635: 
 636:         if collect_week_data:
 637:             week_data.append({
 638:                 "signal_date": monday_ts.date() if hasattr(monday_ts, 'date') else monday_ts,
 639:                 "direction": direction,

 340:         # f4 EMBI acceleration z (identical to screen)
 341:         embi_ser = embi.loc[:asof].dropna()
 342:         embi_acc = (float((embi_ser.diff(5).iloc[-1] - embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).mean())
 343:                           / (embi_ser.diff(5).tail(252).std() + 1e-9))
 344:                     if len(embi_ser) > 260 else np.nan)
 345:         # f5 RESINT z with 45d publication lag (identical to screen)
 346:         res_ser = resint.loc[:asof - pd.Timedelta(days=45)].dropna()
 347:         res_z = (float((res_ser.iloc[-1] - res_ser.tail(12).mean())
 348:                        / (res_ser.tail(12).std() + 1e-9))
 349:                  if len(res_ser) > 12 else np.nan)
 350:         # f3 Monday gap-tail conditional prob given VIX tercile (identical to screen)
 351:         vix_t1 = float(vix.loc[:asof].iloc[-1]) if len(vix.loc[:asof]) else np.nan
 352:         prev_mon = prev[prev["dow"] == 0].tail(252)
 353:         if len(prev_mon) > 30 and not np.isnan(vix_t1):
 354:             vix_al = vix.reindex(prev_mon["date"], method="ffill")
 355:             ter = pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, labels=False, duplicates="drop")
 356:             my_ter = 1
 357:             if len(pd.unique(ter.dropna())) == 3:
 358:                 _cut = pd.cut([vix_t1], bins=pd.qcut(vix_al, 3, retbins=True,
 359:                               duplicates="drop")[1], labels=False, include_lowest=True)[0]
 360:                 if pd.isna(_cut):
 361:                     _cut = 0 if vix_t1 < float(vix_al.min()) else 2
 362:                 my_ter = int(_cut)
 363:             mask = (ter == my_ter).to_numpy()
 364:             thr = prev_mon["gap"].quantile(0.90)
 365:             f3 = float((prev_mon["gap"].to_numpy()[mask] > thr).mean()) if mask.sum() > 5 else np.nan
 366:         else:
 367:             f3 = np.nan
 368:         # persistence: q90 of the trailing 252 realized-5d-range values strictly < t
 369:         rng5_hist = prev["rng5"].dropna().tail(252)
 370:         pers = float(rng5_hist.quantile(0.90)) if len(rng5_hist) >= 252 else np.nan
 371:         rows.append({"monday": wk.start_time, "last_date": g["date"].iloc[-1],
 372:                      "range_pct": float(range_pct), "persistence": pers,
 373:                      "f2": float(vv) if np.isfinite(vv) else np.nan,
 374:                      "f3": f3, "f4": embi_acc, "f5": res_z})
 375:     W = pd.DataFrame(rows).sort_values("monday").reset_index(drop=True)
 376: 
 377:     feat_cols = ["f2", "f3", "f4", "f5"]
 378:     design_start = pd.Timestamp("2020-01-01")
 379:     design_end = pd.Timestamp("2025-01-01")
 380:     ceilings, q90_hist = {}, []          # q90_hist: (monday, q90_hat) for the causal median
 381:     n_qr_fallback = n_inactive = n_weeks = 0
 382:     for i in range(len(W)):
 383:         t = W["monday"].iloc[i]
 384:         if t < design_start:
 385:             continue
 386:         n_weeks += 1
 387:         row_feats = W[feat_cols].iloc[i].to_numpy(dtype=float)
 388:         train = W[(W["last_date"] < t) & W["range_pct"].notna()].dropna(subset=feat_cols)
 389:         qr_pred = np.nan
 390:         if np.isfinite(row_feats).all() and len(train) >= 52:
 391:             try:
 392:                 X = sm.add_constant(train[feat_cols].to_numpy(dtype=float))
 393:                 qr = sm.QuantReg(train["range_pct"].to_numpy(dtype=float), X).fit(q=0.90)
 394:                 qr_pred = float(np.asarray(qr.params) @ np.concatenate(([1.0], row_feats)))
 395:             except Exception:
 396:                 qr_pred = np.nan
 397:         pers = W["persistence"].iloc[i]
 398:         if np.isfinite(pers) and np.isfinite(qr_pred):
 399:             q90_hat = 0.5 * pers + 0.5 * qr_pred
 400:         elif np.isfinite(pers):
 401:             q90_hat = float(pers)
 402:             n_qr_fallback += 1
 403:         else:
 404:             q90_hat = np.nan
 405:         # causal design median: q90_hat values with design_start <= s < min(t, design_end)
 406:         med_vals = [v for (s, v) in q90_hist if s < min(t, design_end) and np.isfinite(v)]
 407:         if np.isfinite(q90_hat):
 408:             q90_hist.append((t, q90_hat))
 409:         if len(med_vals) >= 26 and np.isfinite(q90_hat) and q90_hat > 0:
 410:             med = float(np.median(med_vals))
 411:             ceilings[t] = 1.5 * float(np.clip(med / q90_hat, 0.5, 1.0))
 412:         else:
 413:             ceilings[t] = 1.5   # warm-up / fail-safe: inactive, never raises above 1.5
 414:             n_inactive += 1
 415:     meta = {
 416:         "n_weeks": n_weeks, "n_qr_fallback": n_qr_fallback, "n_ceiling_inactive": n_inactive,
 417:         "median_design_final": (float(np.median([v for (s, v) in q90_hist
 418:                                                  if s < design_end and np.isfinite(v)]))
 419:                                 if q90_hist else None),
 420:         "ceiling_mean": float(np.mean(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
 421:         "ceiling_min": float(np.min(list(ceilings.values()))) if ceilings else None,
 422:     }
 423:     return ceilings, meta

 427: # Phase 1: Walk-forward backtest (Dashboard â€” OOS 2025)
 428: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 429: 
 430: def _run_weekly_loop(df, feature_cols, cfg, year, collect_week_data=False):
 431:     """
 432:     Smart Simple v3.0 core loop â€” Momentum Adaptativo + RÃ©gimen Gate.
 433: 
 434:     NO ML model. Signal = multi-horizon momentum (ret_10d/20d/50d).
 435:     Architecture:
 436:       Layer 1: Regime gate (Hurst) â†’ decides WHETHER to trade
 437:       Layer 2: Momentum signal â†’ decides WHICH direction
 438:       Layer 3: Execution (Half-Kelly, ATR TP, Effective HS, CB, DL)
 439:     """
 440:     test_start = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-01-01")
 441:     test_end = pd.Timestamp(f"{year}-12-31")
 442:     test_data = df[(df["date"] >= test_start) & (df["date"] <= test_end)].copy()
 443:     test_data["dow"] = test_data["date"].dt.dayofweek
 444:     mondays = sorted(test_data[test_data["dow"] == 0]["date"].unique())
 445: 
 446:     if not len(mondays):

 450:     equity = 10000.0
===== 590-670 =====
 590:         )
 591: 
 592:         pnl = compute_pnl(direction, entry, exit_p, final_lev, reason,
 593:                            cfg["maker_fee"], cfg["slippage"])
 594:

 596:         equity *= (1 + pnl)
 597:         peak_equity = max(peak_equity, equity)
 598:         recent_pnls.append(pnl * 100)
 599: 
 600:         # Circuit breaker update
 601:         if pnl < 0:
 602:             consecutive_losses += 1
 603:         else:

 605:             if cb_active:

 608: 
 609:         trade_id += 1
 610:         exit_dates = [d for d in week_dates if not df[df["date"] == d].empty]

 612: 
 613:         trade_dict = {
 614:             "trade_id": trade_id,
 615:             "timestamp": datetime(entry_date.year, entry_date.month, entry_date.day,
 616:                                   9, 0, 0, tzinfo=COT).isoformat(),

 623:             "pnl_pct": round(pnl * 100, 4),
 624:             "exit_reason": reason,
 625:             "equity_at_entry": round(equity_at_entry, 2),

 629:             "hard_stop_pct": round(effective_hs * 100, 2),
 630:             "take_profit_pct": round(effective_tp * 100, 2),
 631:             "regime": regime.state.value,

 633:         }
 634:         trades.append(trade_dict)
 635: 
 636:         if collect_week_data:
 637:             week_data.append({

   1: """
   2: Forecasting Dataset Loader (SSOT)
   3: =================================
   4: 
   5: Single shared data loading + feature building for all forecasting consumers.
   6: DB-first with parquet fallback.
   7: 
   8: Replaces duplicated load_data() / load_full_dataset() / load_and_build_features()
   9: across 4 files with ONE implementation.
  10: 
  11: Usage:
  12:     from src.forecasting.ssot_config import ForecastingSSOTConfig
  13:     from src.forecasting.dataset_loader import ForecastingDatasetLoader
  14: 
  15:     cfg = ForecastingSSOTConfig.load()
  16:     loader = ForecastingDatasetLoader(cfg)
  17:     df, feature_cols = loader.load_dataset(target_horizon=5)
  18: 
  19: Contract: CTR-FORECAST-DATA-LOADER-001
  20: Version: 1.0.0
  21: """
  22: 
  23: import logging
  24: import os
  25: from pathlib import Path
  26: 
  27: import numpy as np
  28: import pandas as pd
  29: 
  30: from src.forecasting.ssot_config import ForecastingSSOTConfig
  31: 
  32: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
  33: 
  34: 
  35: def _find_project_root() -> Path:
  36:     """Walk up from this file to find project root (contains pyproject.toml)."""
  37:     p = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  38:     while p != p.parent:
  39:         if (p / "pyproject.toml").is_file():
  40:             return p
  41:         p = p.parent
  42:     airflow_root = Path("/opt/airflow")
  43:     if airflow_root.exists():
  44:         return airflow_root
  45:     return Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
  46: 
  47: 
  48: class ForecastingDatasetLoader:
  49:     """DB-first data loader with parquet fallback. Builds 21 SSOT features."""
  50: 
  51:     def __init__(
  52:         self,
  53:         config: ForecastingSSOTConfig,
  54:         db_url: str | None = None,
  55:         project_root: Path | None = None,
  56:     ):
  57:         self.config = config
  58:         self.db_url = db_url or os.environ.get("DATABASE_URL")
  59:         self.project_root = project_root or _find_project_root()
  60: 
  61:     def load_dataset(
  62:         self,
  63:         target_horizon: int | None = None,
  64:     ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str]]:
  65:         """
  66:         Load OHLCV + macro, build features, optionally compute target.
  67: 
  68:         Data source priority:
  69:         1. PostgreSQL (db_url set and DB reachable)
  70:         2. Parquet fallback (always works)
  71: 
  72:         Args:
  73:             target_horizon: If set, compute target column for this horizon.
  74:                            None = no target column (e.g., for inference).
  75: 
  76:         Returns:
  77:             (df, feature_columns) where df has all feature columns built.
  78:         """
  79:         # Load OHLCV
  80:         df_ohlcv = self._load_ohlcv()
  81: 
  82:         # Load macro
  83:         df_macro = self._load_macro()
  84: 
  85:         # Merge macro -> OHLCV with T-1 anti-leakage
  86:         df = self._merge_macro(df_ohlcv, df_macro)
  87: 
  88:         # Build 21 SSOT features
  89:         df = self._build_features(df)
  90: 
  91:         # Compute target if requested
  92:         if target_horizon is not None:
  93:             col_name = f"target_return_{target_horizon}d"
  94:             df[col_name] = np.log(df["close"].shift(-target_horizon) / df["close"])
  95: 
  96:         feature_cols = list(self.config.get_feature_columns())
  97:         return df, feature_cols
  98: 
  99:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 100:     # OHLCV loading
 101:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 102: 
 103:     def _load_ohlcv(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
 104:         """Load daily OHLCV. Try DB first, fall back to parquet."""
 105:         df = self._load_ohlcv_from_db()
 106:         if df is not None and len(df) > 0:
 107:             return df
 108:         return self._load_ohlcv_from_parquet()
 109: 
 110:     def _load_ohlcv_from_db(self) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
 111:         """Try loading OHLCV from PostgreSQL."""
 112:         if not self.db_url:
 113:             return None
 114: 
 115:         try:
 116:             import psycopg2
 117: 
 118:             ohlcv_cfg = self.config.get_data_source("ohlcv")
 119:             # Per-asset opt-out: some assets (e.g. BTC in the mixed-symbol
 120:             # asset_daily_ohlcv table) must always use the parquet seed.
 121:             if ohlcv_cfg.get("disable_db"):
 122:                 return None
 123:             table = ohlcv_cfg["db_table"]
 124:             cols = ohlcv_cfg["db_columns"]
 125:             col_str = ", ".join(cols)
 126: 
 127:             conn = psycopg2.connect(self.db_url)
 128:             try:
 129:                 cur = conn.cursor()
 130:                 cur.execute(f"SELECT {col_str} FROM {table} ORDER BY date ASC")
 131:                 rows = cur.fetchall()
 132:             finally:
 133:                 conn.close()
 134: 
 135:             if not rows:
 136:                 logger.info("[DataLoader] DB OHLCV table empty, falling back to parquet")
 137:                 return None
 138: 
 139:             df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=cols)
 140:             df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"])
 141:             for c in ["open", "high", "low", "close"]:
 142:                 df[c] = df[c].astype(float)
 143:             df = df.sort_values("date").drop_duplicates(subset=["date"]).reset_index(drop=True)
 144:             logger.info(f"[DataLoader] OHLCV from DB: {len(df)} rows, "
 145:                         f"{df['date'].iloc[0].date()} to {df['date'].iloc[-1].date()}")
 146:             return df
 147: 
 148:         except Exception as e:
 149:             logger.warning(f"[DataLoader] DB OHLCV failed ({e}), falling back to parquet")
 150:             return None
 151: 
 152:     def _load_ohlcv_from_parquet(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
 153:         """Load OHLCV from parquet seed file."""
 154:         ohlcv_cfg = self.config.get_data_source("ohlcv")
 155:         parquet_path = self.project_root / ohlcv_cfg["fallback_parquet"]
 156: 
 157:         if not parquet_path.exists():
 158:             raise FileNotFoundError(f"[DataLoader] Daily OHLCV not found: {parquet_path}")
 159: 
 160:         time_col = ohlcv_cfg.get("parquet_time_column", "time")
 161:         df = pd.read_parquet(parquet_path).reset_index()
 162:         if time_col in df.columns and "date" not in df.columns:
 163:             df.rename(columns={time_col: "date"}, inplace=True)
 164:         df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"]).dt.tz_localize(None).dt.normalize()
 165:         df = df[["date", "open", "high", "low", "close"]].copy()
 166:         df = df.sort_values("date").drop_duplicates(subset=["date"]).reset_index(drop=True)
 167: 
 168:         logger.info(f"[DataLoader] OHLCV from parquet: {len(df)} rows, "
 169:                     f"{df['date'].iloc[0].date()} to {df['date'].iloc[-1].date()}")
 170:         return df
 171: 
 172:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 173:     # Macro loading
 174:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 175: 
 176:     def _load_macro(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
 177:         """Load macro data. Try DB first, fall back to parquet."""
 178:         df = self._load_macro_from_db()
 179:         if df is not None and len(df) > 0:
 180:             return df
 181:         return self._load_macro_from_parquet()
 182: 
 183:     def _load_macro_from_db(self) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
 184:         """Try loading macro from PostgreSQL."""
 185:         if not self.db_url:
 186:             return None
 187: 
 188:         try:
 189:             import psycopg2
 190: 
 191:             macro_cfg = self.config.get_data_source("macro")
 192:             table = macro_cfg["db_table"]
 193:             date_col = macro_cfg["db_date_column"]
 194:             col_mapping = self.config.get_macro_column_mapping()
 195: 
 196:             # Build SQL: select date + raw macro columns
 197:             raw_cols = list(col_mapping.keys())
 198:             col_str = ", ".join([date_col] + raw_cols)
 199: 
 200:             conn = psycopg2.connect(self.db_url)
 201:             try:
 202:                 cur = conn.cursor()
 203:                 cur.execute(f"SELECT {col_str} FROM {table} ORDER BY {date_col} ASC")
 204:                 rows = cur.fetchall()
 205:             finally:
 206:                 conn.close()
 207: 
 208:             if not rows:
 209:                 logger.info("[DataLoader] DB macro table empty, falling back to parquet")
 210:                 return None
 211: 
 212:             df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=[date_col] + raw_cols)
 213:             df.rename(columns={date_col: "date"}, inplace=True)
 214:             df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"])
 215:             df.rename(columns=col_mapping, inplace=True)
 216:             df = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
 217: 
 218:             logger.info(f"[DataLoader] Macro from DB: {len(df)} rows, "
 219:                         f"{len(col_mapping)} variables")
 220:             return df
 221: 
 222:         except Exception as e:
 223:             logger.warning(f"[DataLoader] DB macro failed ({e}), falling back to parquet")
 224:             return None
 225: 
 226:     def _load_macro_from_parquet(self) -> pd.DataFrame:
 227:         """Load macro from parquet seed file."""
 228:         macro_cfg = self.config.get_data_source("macro")
 229:         parquet_path = self.project_root / macro_cfg["fallback_parquet"]
 230: 
 231:         if not parquet_path.exists():
 232:             raise FileNotFoundError(f"[DataLoader] Macro data not found: {parquet_path}")
 233: 
 234:         col_mapping = self.config.get_macro_column_mapping()
 235: 
 236:         df = pd.read_parquet(parquet_path).reset_index()
 237:         # First column is the date (may be named 'fecha', 'date', or index)
 238:         df.rename(columns={df.columns[0]: "date"}, inplace=True)
 239:         df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"]).dt.tz_localize(None).dt.normalize()
 240: 
 241:         # Select only the raw columns we need + rename
 242:         available_raw = [c for c in col_mapping.keys() if c in df.columns]
 243:         if not available_raw:
 244:             raise ValueError(
 245:                 f"[DataLoader] No macro columns found. Expected: {list(col_mapping.keys())}, "
 246:                 f"available: {list(df.columns[:20])}"
 247:             )
 248: 
 249:         df_sub = df[["date"] + available_raw].copy()
 250:         df_sub.rename(columns=col_mapping, inplace=True)
 251:         df_sub = df_sub.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
 252: 
 253:         logger.info(f"[DataLoader] Macro from parquet: {len(df_sub)} rows, "
 254:                     f"{len(available_raw)}/{len(col_mapping)} variables")
 255:         return df_sub
 256: 
 257:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 258:     # Macro merge (anti-leakage)
 259:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 260: 
 261:     def _merge_macro(self, df_ohlcv: pd.DataFrame, df_macro: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
 262:         """
 263:         Merge macro into OHLCV with anti-leakage:
 264:         1. shift(1) on macro columns = T-1 (use yesterday's macro)
 265:         2. merge_asof(direction='backward') = no forward fill from future
 266:         """
 267:         macro_feature_cols = list(self.config.get_macro_column_mapping().values())
 268: 
 269:         # T-1 shift for anti-leakage
 270:         for col in macro_feature_cols:
 271:             if col in df_macro.columns:
 272:                 df_macro[col] = df_macro[col].shift(1)
 273: 
 274:         df = pd.merge_asof(
 275:             df_ohlcv.sort_values("date"),
 276:             df_macro.sort_values("date"),
 277:             on="date",
 278:             direction="backward",
 279:         )
 280:         return df
 281: 
 282:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 283:     # Feature building (THE single copy)
 284:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 285: 
 286:     def _build_features(self, df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
 287:         """
 288:         Build 21 SSOT features from raw OHLCV + macro.
 289: 
 290:         Features:
 291:             4 price:     close, open, high, low (already in df)
 292:             4 returns:   return_1d, return_5d, return_10d, return_20d
 293:             3 volatility: volatility_5d, volatility_10d, volatility_20d
 294:             3 technical: rsi_14d (Wilder's EMA), ma_ratio_20d, ma_ratio_50d
 295:             3 calendar:  day_of_week, month, is_month_end
 296:             4 macro:     dxy_close_lag1, oil_close_lag1, vix_close_lag1, embi_close_lag1
 297:         """
 298:         df = df.copy()
 299: 
 300:         # Returns (4)
 301:         df["return_1d"] = df["close"].pct_change(1)
 302:         df["return_5d"] = df["close"].pct_change(5)
 303:         df["return_10d"] = df["close"].pct_change(10)
 304:         df["return_20d"] = df["close"].pct_change(20)
 305: 
 306:         # Volatility (3)
 307:         df["volatility_5d"] = df["return_1d"].rolling(5).std()
 308:         df["volatility_10d"] = df["return_1d"].rolling(10).std()
 309:         df["volatility_20d"] = df["return_1d"].rolling(20).std()
 310: 
 311:         # RSI 14d â€” Wilder's EMA (alpha=1/period, NOT pandas default)
 312:         delta = df["close"].diff()
 313:         gain = delta.where(delta > 0, 0.0)
 314:         loss = -delta.where(delta < 0, 0.0)
 315:         avg_gain = gain.ewm(alpha=1 / 14, min_periods=14).mean()
 316:         avg_loss = loss.ewm(alpha=1 / 14, min_periods=14).mean()
 317:         rs = avg_gain / avg_loss.replace(0, np.nan)
 318:         df["rsi_14d"] = 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))
 319: 
 320:         # MA ratios (2)
 321:         df["ma_ratio_20d"] = df["close"] / df["close"].rolling(20).mean()
 322:         df["ma_ratio_50d"] = df["close"] / df["close"].rolling(50).mean()
 323: 
 324:         # Calendar (3)
 325:         df["day_of_week"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"]).dt.dayofweek
 326:         df["month"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"]).dt.month
 327:         df["is_month_end"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"]).dt.is_month_end.astype(int)
 328: 
 329:         # Macro (4) â€” forward-fill gaps after merge
 330:         macro_cols = list(self.config.get_macro_column_mapping().values())
 331:         for col in macro_cols:
 332:             if col in df.columns:
 333:                 df[col] = df[col].ffill()
 334: 
 335:         return df
 336: 
 337:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 338:     # Convenience: load with DB extension (for DAGs)
 339:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 340: 
 341:     def load_dataset_with_db_extension(
 342:         self,
 343:         target_horizon: int | None = None,
 344:         db_conn_func=None,
 345:     ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, list[str]]:
 346:         """
 347:         Load from parquet, then extend with latest DB rows (if available).
 348:         Used by Airflow DAGs that have DB access and want the freshest data.
 349: 
 350:         Args:
 351:             target_horizon: Horizon for target column.
 352:             db_conn_func: Callable returning a psycopg2 connection.
 353:                          If None, uses self.db_url.
 354:         """
 355:         # Load base from parquet (always available)
 356:         df_ohlcv = self._load_ohlcv_from_parquet()
 357: 
 358:         # Try extending with DB rows newer than parquet
 359:         if db_conn_func is not None or self.db_url:
 360:             try:

===== scripts/data/resample_1h_to_daily.py =====
   1: """
   2: Resample hourly OHLCV seed to daily bars.
   3: 
   4: Usage:
   5:     python scripts/resample_1h_to_daily.py
   6: 
   7: Input:  seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet (6,858 rows, hourly)
   8: Output: seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet (~1,399 rows, daily)
   9: """
  10: import pandas as pd
  11: import numpy as np
  12: from pathlib import Path
  13: 
  14: def main():
  15:     project_root = Path(__file__).parent.parent
  16:     input_path = project_root / "seeds" / "latest" / "usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet"
  17:     output_path = project_root / "seeds" / "latest" / "usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet"
  18: 
  19:     print(f"Loading hourly OHLCV from {input_path}")
  20:     df = pd.read_parquet(input_path)
  21:     print(f"  Rows: {len(df)}, Range: {df.index.min()} -> {df.index.max()}")
  22: 
  23:     # Resample to daily: standard OHLCV aggregation
  24:     daily = df.resample('1D').agg({
  25:         'open': 'first',
  26:         'high': 'max',
  27:         'low': 'min',
  28:         'close': 'last',
  29:         'volume': 'sum',
  30:     }).dropna(subset=['open'])  # Drop days with no data (weekends/holidays)
  31: 
  32:     # Preserve symbol column
  33:     daily['symbol'] = 'USD/COP'
  34: 
  35:     # Validation
  36:     print(f"\nDaily OHLCV: {len(daily)} rows")
  37:     print(f"  Range: {daily.index.min()} -> {daily.index.max()}")
  38:     print(f"  NaN per column: {daily.isna().sum().to_dict()}")
  39: 
  40:     # No weekends
  41:     weekday_mask = daily.index.dayofweek < 5
  42:     weekend_count = (~weekday_mask).sum()
  43:     if weekend_count > 0:
  44:         print(f"  WARNING: {weekend_count} weekend rows, removing...")
  45:         daily = daily[weekday_mask]
  46: 
  47:     # OHLC sanity
  48:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['low']).all(), "high < low found!"
  49:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['open']).all(), "high < open found!"
  50:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['close']).all(), "high < close found!"
  51: 
  52:     # No NaN in OHLC
  53:     assert daily[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']].isna().sum().sum() == 0, "NaN in OHLC!"
  54: 
  55:     # Price range check
  56:     prices = daily[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']]
  57:     print(f"  Price range: {prices.min().min():.2f} - {prices.max().max():.2f}")
  58:     assert prices.min().min() > 2000, "Price too low for USDCOP"
  59:     assert prices.max().max() < 6000, "Price too high for USDCOP"
  60: 
  61:     # Save
  62:     daily.to_parquet(output_path)
  63:     print(f"\nSaved to {output_path}")
  64:     print(f"  {len(daily)} daily bars, {len(daily) / 252:.1f} years of data")
  65: 
  66:     # Show yearly bar counts
  67:     yearly = daily.groupby(daily.index.year).size()
  68:     print(f"\nBars per year:")
  69:     for year, count in yearly.items():
  70:         print(f"  {year}: {count}")
  71: 
  72: 
  73: if __name__ == "__main__":
  74:     main()
===== scripts/data/resample_5m_to_1h.py =====
   1: """
   2: Resample 5-minute OHLCV to 1-hour bars for USDCOP.
   3: 
   4: Session: 8:00-12:55 COT = 5 hourly bars per day (8:xx, 9:xx, 10:xx, 11:xx, 12:xx)
   5: Output: seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet
   6: 
   7: Usage:
   8:     python scripts/resample_5m_to_1h.py
   9: """
  10: 
  11: import sys
  12: from pathlib import Path
  13: 
  14: import numpy as np
  15: import pandas as pd
  16: 
  17: PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
  18: sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
  19: 
  20: 
  21: def resample_5m_to_1h(input_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
  22:     """Resample 5-min OHLCV to 1-hour bars."""
  23:     print(f"Loading 5-min data from {input_path}...")
  24:     df = pd.read_parquet(input_path)
  25: 
  26:     # Ensure datetime index
  27:     if 'time' in df.columns:
  28:         df['time'] = pd.to_datetime(df['time'])
  29:         df = df.set_index('time')
  30:     elif 'datetime' in df.columns:
  31:         df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['datetime'])
  32:         df = df.set_index('datetime')
  33: 
  34:     # Filter to USD/COP only if symbol column exists
  35:     if 'symbol' in df.columns:
  36:         df = df[df['symbol'] == 'USD/COP'].copy()
  37:         df = df.drop(columns=['symbol'])
  38: 
  39:     # Remove timezone for resampling, then re-add
  40:     original_tz = df.index.tz
  41:     if original_tz is not None:
  42:         df.index = df.index.tz_localize(None)
  43: 
  44:     df = df.sort_index()
  45:     print(f"  Input: {len(df)} rows, {df.index.min()} to {df.index.max()}")
  46: 
  47:     # Standard OHLCV resampling to 1H
  48:     agg = {
  49:         'open': 'first',
  50:         'high': 'max',
  51:         'low': 'min',
  52:         'close': 'last',
  53:     }
  54:     if 'volume' in df.columns:
  55:         agg['volume'] = 'sum'
  56: 
  57:     df_1h = df.resample('1h').agg(agg).dropna(subset=['close'])
  58: 
  59:     # Filter to session hours [8, 9, 10, 11, 12] COT
  60:     session_hours = [8, 9, 10, 11, 12]
  61:     df_1h = df_1h[df_1h.index.hour.isin(session_hours)]
  62: 
  63:     # Remove weekends
  64:     df_1h = df_1h[df_1h.index.dayofweek < 5]
  65: 
  66:     # Re-add timezone
  67:     if original_tz is not None:
  68:         df_1h.index = df_1h.index.tz_localize(original_tz)
  69: 
  70:     # Add symbol column
  71:     df_1h['symbol'] = 'USD/COP'
  72: 
  73:     # Rename index
  74:     df_1h.index.name = 'time'
  75: 
  76:     # Validate
  77:     print(f"\n  Output: {len(df_1h)} rows")
  78:     print(f"  Date range: {df_1h.index.min()} to {df_1h.index.max()}")
  79: 
  80:     hours = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).hour if df_1h.index.tz else df_1h.index.hour
  81:     assert all(h in session_hours for h in hours), f"Hours outside session: {set(hours) - set(session_hours)}"
  82: 
  83:     weekdays = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).dayofweek if df_1h.index.tz else df_1h.index.dayofweek
  84:     assert all(d < 5 for d in weekdays), "Weekend bars found!"
  85: 
  86:     assert df_1h[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']].isna().sum().sum() == 0, "NaN in OHLC!"
  87:     assert (df_1h['high'] >= df_1h['low']).all(), "high < low found!"
  88: 
  89:     # Bars per day stats
  90:     if df_1h.index.tz:
  91:         dates = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).date
  92:     else:
  93:         dates = df_1h.index.date
  94:     bars_per_day = pd.Series(dates).value_counts()
  95:     print(f"  Median bars/day: {bars_per_day.median():.0f}")
  96:     print(f"  Mean bars/day: {bars_per_day.mean():.1f}")
  97: 
  98:     # Price range sanity
  99:     print(f"  Price range: {df_1h['close'].min():.2f} - {df_1h['close'].max():.2f}")
 100: 
 101:     # Save
 102:     output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 103:     df_1h.to_parquet(output_path)
 104:     print(f"\n  Saved to {output_path}")
 105: 
 106:     return df_1h
 107: 
 108: 
 109: if __name__ == "__main__":
 110:     input_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_m5_ohlcv.parquet"
 111:     output_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet"
 112: 
 113:     df_1h = resample_5m_to_1h(input_path, output_path)
 114:     print(f"\nDone! {len(df_1h)} hourly bars ready.")

===== scripts/data/resample_1h_to_daily.py =====
   1: """
   2: Resample hourly OHLCV seed to daily bars.
   3: 
   4: Usage:
   5:     python scripts/resample_1h_to_daily.py
   6: 
   7: Input:  seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet (6,858 rows, hourly)
   8: Output: seeds/latest/usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet (~1,399 rows, daily)
   9: """
  10: import pandas as pd
  11: import numpy as np
  12: from pathlib import Path
  13: 
  14: def main():
  15:     project_root = Path(__file__).parent.parent
  16:     input_path = project_root / "seeds" / "latest" / "usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet"
  17:     output_path = project_root / "seeds" / "latest" / "usdcop_daily_ohlcv.parquet"
  18: 
  19:     print(f"Loading hourly OHLCV from {input_path}")
  20:     df = pd.read_parquet(input_path)
  21:     print(f"  Rows: {len(df)}, Range: {df.index.min()} -> {df.index.max()}")
  22: 
  23:     # Resample to daily: standard OHLCV aggregation
  24:     daily = df.resample('1D').agg({
  25:         'open': 'first',
  26:         'high': 'max',
  27:         'low': 'min',
  28:         'close': 'last',
  29:         'volume': 'sum',
  30:     }).dropna(subset=['open'])  # Drop days with no data (weekends/holidays)
  31: 
  32:     # Preserve symbol column
  33:     daily['symbol'] = 'USD/COP'
  34: 
  35:     # Validation
  36:     print(f"\nDaily OHLCV: {len(daily)} rows")
  37:     print(f"  Range: {daily.index.min()} -> {daily.index.max()}")
  38:     print(f"  NaN per column: {daily.isna().sum().to_dict()}")
  39: 
  40:     # No weekends
  41:     weekday_mask = daily.index.dayofweek < 5
  42:     weekend_count = (~weekday_mask).sum()
  43:     if weekend_count > 0:
  44:         print(f"  WARNING: {weekend_count} weekend rows, removing...")
  45:         daily = daily[weekday_mask]
  46: 
  47:     # OHLC sanity
  48:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['low']).all(), "high < low found!"
  49:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['open']).all(), "high < open found!"
  50:     assert (daily['high'] >= daily['close']).all(), "high < close found!"
  51: 
  52:     # No NaN in OHLC
  53:     assert daily[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']].isna().sum().sum() == 0, "NaN in OHLC!"
  54: 
  55:     # Price range check
  56:     prices = daily[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']]
  57:     print(f"  Price range: {prices.min().min():.2f} - {prices.max().max():.2f}")
  58:     assert prices.min().min() > 2000, "Price too low for USDCOP"
  59:     assert prices.max().max() < 6000, "Price too high for USDCOP"
  60: 
  61:     # Save
  62:     daily.to_parquet(output_path)
  63:     print(f"\nSaved to {output_path}")
  64:     print(f"  {len(daily)} daily bars, {len(daily) / 252:.1f} years of data")
  65: 
  66:     # Show yearly bar counts
  67:     yearly = daily.groupby(daily.index.year).size()
  68:     print(f"\nBars per year:")
  69:     for year, count in yearly.items():
  70:         print(f"  {year}: {count}")
  71: 
  72: 
  73: if __name__ == "__main__":
  74:     main()
===== scripts/data/resample_5m_to_1h.py =====
   1: """
   2: Resample 5-minute OHLCV to 1-hour bars for USDCOP.
   3: 
   4: Session: 8:00-12:55 COT = 5 hourly bars per day (8:xx, 9:xx, 10:xx, 11:xx, 12:xx)
   5: Output: seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet
   6: 
   7: Usage:
   8:     python scripts/resample_5m_to_1h.py
   9: """
  10: 
  11: import sys
  12: from pathlib import Path
  13: 
  14: import numpy as np
  15: import pandas as pd
  16: 
  17: PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent
  18: sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
  19: 
  20: 
  21: def resample_5m_to_1h(input_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
  22:     """Resample 5-min OHLCV to 1-hour bars."""
  23:     print(f"Loading 5-min data from {input_path}...")
  24:     df = pd.read_parquet(input_path)
  25: 
  26:     # Ensure datetime index
  27:     if 'time' in df.columns:
  28:         df['time'] = pd.to_datetime(df['time'])
  29:         df = df.set_index('time')
  30:     elif 'datetime' in df.columns:
  31:         df['datetime'] = pd.to_datetime(df['datetime'])
  32:         df = df.set_index('datetime')
  33: 
  34:     # Filter to USD/COP only if symbol column exists
  35:     if 'symbol' in df.columns:
  36:         df = df[df['symbol'] == 'USD/COP'].copy()
  37:         df = df.drop(columns=['symbol'])
  38: 
  39:     # Remove timezone for resampling, then re-add
  40:     original_tz = df.index.tz
  41:     if original_tz is not None:
  42:         df.index = df.index.tz_localize(None)
  43: 
  44:     df = df.sort_index()
  45:     print(f"  Input: {len(df)} rows, {df.index.min()} to {df.index.max()}")
  46: 
  47:     # Standard OHLCV resampling to 1H
  48:     agg = {
  49:         'open': 'first',
  50:         'high': 'max',
  51:         'low': 'min',
  52:         'close': 'last',
  53:     }
  54:     if 'volume' in df.columns:
  55:         agg['volume'] = 'sum'
  56: 
  57:     df_1h = df.resample('1h').agg(agg).dropna(subset=['close'])
  58: 
  59:     # Filter to session hours [8, 9, 10, 11, 12] COT
  60:     session_hours = [8, 9, 10, 11, 12]
  61:     df_1h = df_1h[df_1h.index.hour.isin(session_hours)]
  62: 
  63:     # Remove weekends
  64:     df_1h = df_1h[df_1h.index.dayofweek < 5]
  65: 
  66:     # Re-add timezone
  67:     if original_tz is not None:
  68:         df_1h.index = df_1h.index.tz_localize(original_tz)
  69: 
  70:     # Add symbol column
  71:     df_1h['symbol'] = 'USD/COP'
  72: 
  73:     # Rename index
  74:     df_1h.index.name = 'time'
  75: 
  76:     # Validate
  77:     print(f"\n  Output: {len(df_1h)} rows")
  78:     print(f"  Date range: {df_1h.index.min()} to {df_1h.index.max()}")
  79: 
  80:     hours = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).hour if df_1h.index.tz else df_1h.index.hour
  81:     assert all(h in session_hours for h in hours), f"Hours outside session: {set(hours) - set(session_hours)}"
  82: 
  83:     weekdays = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).dayofweek if df_1h.index.tz else df_1h.index.dayofweek
  84:     assert all(d < 5 for d in weekdays), "Weekend bars found!"
  85: 
  86:     assert df_1h[['open', 'high', 'low', 'close']].isna().sum().sum() == 0, "NaN in OHLC!"
  87:     assert (df_1h['high'] >= df_1h['low']).all(), "high < low found!"
  88: 
  89:     # Bars per day stats
  90:     if df_1h.index.tz:
  91:         dates = df_1h.index.tz_localize(None).date
  92:     else:
  93:         dates = df_1h.index.date
  94:     bars_per_day = pd.Series(dates).value_counts()
  95:     print(f"  Median bars/day: {bars_per_day.median():.0f}")
  96:     print(f"  Mean bars/day: {bars_per_day.mean():.1f}")
  97: 
  98:     # Price range sanity
  99:     print(f"  Price range: {df_1h['close'].min():.2f} - {df_1h['close'].max():.2f}")
 100: 
 101:     # Save
 102:     output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 103:     df_1h.to_parquet(output_path)
 104:     print(f"\n  Saved to {output_path}")
 105: 
 106:     return df_1h
 107: 
 108: 
 109: if __name__ == "__main__":
 110:     input_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_m5_ohlcv.parquet"
 111:     output_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "seeds/latest/usdcop_1h_ohlcv.parquet"
 112: 
 113:     df_1h = resample_5m_to_1h(input_path, output_path)
 114:     print(f"\nDone! {len(df_1h)} hourly bars ready.")

 300:     if sess.mode == "exchange_hours" and sess.open and sess.close:
 301:         oh, om = map(int, sess.open.split(":"))
 302:         ch, cm = map(int, sess.close.split(":"))
 303:         mins = local.dt.hour * 60 + local.dt.minute
 304:         mask &= (mins >= oh * 60 + om) & (mins <= ch * 60 + cm)
 305:     out = df[mask].copy()
 306:     dropped = len(df) - len(out)
 307:     if dropped:
 308:         log.info("  session filter: dropped %d off-session bars (mode=%s)", dropped, sess.mode)
 309:     return out
 310: 
 311: 
 312: def _daily_to_nyclose(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
 313:     """Normalize daily bars to the NY-close instant (17:00 ET) in UTC (SPEC-02).
 314: 
 315:     The *calendar date* of a daily bar is the trading day it belongs to, and that day is carried
 316:     by the source label in **UTC space**: TwelveData daily bars arrive at 00:00 UTC on the label
 317:     date (see ``_td_fetch``); the Investing cross-check localizes the bare date to ET â†’ 04:00/05:00
 318:     UTC â€” still the same UTC calendar day. So we anchor the date with ``tz_convert("UTC").normalize()``,
 319:     NOT ``tz_convert("America/New_York")``: converting a 00:00-UTC stamp to ET yields 19:00/20:00 of
 320:     the *previous* ET day, and ``normalize`` then snaps every bar back one calendar day (Monâ†’Sun) â€”
 321:     the day-shift bug that piled Gold bars onto Sunday and emptied Friday. Anchoring in UTC is
 322:     correct for both sources because ET-midnight is always the same UTC day (ET = UTC-4/-5, never
 323:     crosses back).
 324: 
 325:     DST-correct: build the *naive* 17:00 wall-clock for the trading date, THEN localize to ET. Adding
 326:     a Timedelta to a tz-aware midnight would add absolute UTC duration and land at 16:00/18:00 ET on
 327:     the two DST-transition days â€” the classic SPEC-02 hazard.
 328:     """
 329:     if df.empty:
 330:         return df
 331:     d = df.copy()
 332:     # Anchor the trading day in UTC (the source-label date), never in ET (see docstring).
 333:     naive_date = d["time"].dt.tz_convert("UTC").dt.normalize().dt.tz_localize(None)
 334:     naive_close = naive_date + pd.Timedelta(hours=17)  # pure wall-clock 17:00 (no DST math)
 335:     d["time"] = (naive_close
 336:                  .dt.tz_localize("America/New_York", nonexistent="shift_forward", ambiguous=True)
 337:                  .dt.tz_convert("UTC"))
 338:     return d.drop_duplicates("time").sort_values("time").reset_index(drop=True)
 339: 
 340: 
 341: def _daily_to_session_close(df: pd.DataFrame, profile) -> pd.DataFrame:
 342:     """Normalize daily labels to the profile's local market-close instant."""
 343:     if df.empty:
 344:         return df
 345:     raw_session = profile.raw.get("session", {})
 346:     close_tz = raw_session.get("daily_close_tz") or profile.session.timezone
 347:     if raw_session.get("daily_close_tz"):
 348:         close_text = raw_session.get("daily_close", "17:00")
 349:     else:
 350:         close_text = profile.session.close or "17:00"
 351:     hour, minute = map(int, close_text.split(":"))
 352:     d = df.copy()
 353:     naive_date = d["time"].dt.tz_convert("UTC").dt.normalize().dt.tz_localize(None)
 354:     naive_close = naive_date + pd.Timedelta(hours=hour, minutes=minute)
 355:     d["time"] = (naive_close
 356:                  .dt.tz_localize(close_tz, nonexistent="shift_forward", ambiguous=True)
 357:                  .dt.tz_convert("UTC"))
 358:     return d.drop_duplicates("time").sort_values("time").reset_index(drop=True)
 359:

 364:     if df.empty:
 365:         rep["problems"].append("EMPTY")
